# Prompt ảnh — 260 cảnh riêng

Công cụ: built-in ImageGen. Một lệnh cho một ảnh; không thay bằng contact sheet. Prompt sửa riêng được ghi tại revisionPromptFile trong storyboard.

## I001 — S001

Một tờ tiền giấy gần như không có giá trị vật liệu, nhưng có thể mua cả một con bò.

Use case: historical-scene. Asset type: one full-bleed landscape 16:9 illustration for a Vietnamese documentary. Primary request: (Mio, a young anime girl guide with short teal bob hair, round glasses, mustard-yellow jacket and carrying a small paper scroll) holds a thin (an unlettered historical paper banknote with an abstract ornamental border, not a modern dollar bill) beside a large ox while (silver bullion ingots or silver coins, used as an alternative store of value) coins sit nearby, introducing the paradox that cheap material can command real goods. Style/medium: 2D anime illustration in the style of a Vox explainer video: clean cel-shaded line art, flat saturated colors, subtle halftone dots and paper-grain texture, editorial infographic composition, cut-out collage layering, warm cream paper background, limited palette of ink navy, vermilion red, imperial gold and jade teal, soft paper-cutout drop shadows, cinematic 16:9 framing, no text, no letters, no numbers, no watermark. Historical setting: Early Chinese market or explicitly abstract historical economic diagram; avoid anachronistic machinery. Composition: one distinct editorial scene, clear focal point, readable visual hierarchy, no contact sheet; keep the bottom 15% and lower-right area comparatively quiet for future captions and channel mascot. Mio, if requested, is a fictional modern guide outside the historical reenactment, with the same teal bob, round glasses and mustard jacket across scenes. Constraints: no text, letters, numerals, readable seals, dates, currency denominations, captions, logos or watermark; natural anatomy; no modern machines in historical scenes. All gauges and flows are qualitative visual metaphors, not measured evidence.

Đích: public/images/tai-sao-tien-giay-thoi-xua-that-bai/001.png
